This makes the taxonomy actionable: model-side failures identify targets for post-training, harness-side failures point to scaffolding and tool-integration fixes, and environment or grader failures reveal evaluation conditions requiring redesign. The schema applies across agent architectures, from coding assistants to long-horizon personal assistants and multi-agent systems. We ground the taxonomy in worked examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories, and evaluate its reproducibility using independent reasoning agents as judges. Across four frontier models, the strongest judge reaches Cohen's $κ=0.76$ against human category labels, suggesting that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences.

</details>


### [101] [Best Friends, Not Forever: Evaluating Long-Horizon Persona Collapse and Behavioral Drift in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28818)
*Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Akshara Prabhakar,Yu Li,Daniel Lee,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI陪伴系统在长期交互中的两大失败模式——"人格崩塌"（角色/边界/价值观/风格丢失）和"行为漂移"（属性逐渐或反复侵蚀），提出ANCHOR可控合成审计框架，对2,008段对话、27个人格、9种交互计划、3种记忆设置、4个模型进行评估，结果显示所有模型和配置均无法可靠保持人格一致性与轨迹记忆（轨迹准确率仅44.4%，用户状态记忆接近随机水平），表明当前系统不支持长期陪伴连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户与AI伴侣进行反复社交互动时，依赖稳定的角色定位和共享历史，但仅保证局部可接受的回复并不能确保角色和历史的一致性长期保持。论文旨在系统化识别并测量"人格崩塌"和"行为漂移"这两个可观测的长期失败模式。

Method: 提出ANCHOR审计框架，包含两个独立探针：Identity Probe（身份探针）采用密封的102项问卷结合逐轮（turn-level）判断，Trajectory Probe（轨迹探针）对35个对话银行中的110个校准反事实问题进行评分。实验覆盖27个人格、9种交互计划、3种生成记忆设置和4个评估模型，共2,008段对话。

Result: 所有评估模型和配置均无法可靠保持人格维度和轨迹维度的持续性：轨迹准确率平均仅44.4%，用户状态记忆接近四选一随机水平；任何上下文条件或记忆设置都无法一致解决这些失败。问卷保留结果因模型和人格侧面而异，与逐轮行为表现不一致，且对评估者选择敏感。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统尚不能可靠支持长期陪伴连续性；审计必须区分人格扮演、轨迹回忆、评估者来源和部署环境等维度，而不是将它们合并为一个单一的信任或稳定性分数。

Abstract: As AI companions increasingly mediate repeated social interaction, users may rely on a stable role and shared history, yet locally acceptable replies do not ensure that either persists. We study two observable long-horizon failures: 'persona collapse', the loss of a deployed role, boundaries, values, or style, and 'behavioral drift', the gradual or recurrent erosion of those properties. We introduce ANCHOR, a controlled synthetic audit that separately measures persona enactment and trajectory recall. The study contains 2,008 conversations spanning 27 personas, nine interaction schedules, three generated memory settings, and four evaluated models. The Identity Probe combines a sealed 102-item questionnaire with turn-level judgments, while the Trajectory Probe scores 110 calibrated counterfactual questions from 35 conversation banks. Our results show that no evaluated model and configuration reliably preserves either dimensions: trajectory accuracy averages only 44.4%, user-state recall remains near four-option chance, and no tested context condition or memory consistently resolves these failures. Questionnaire retention also varies by model and persona facet, disagrees with turn-level behavior, and is sensitive to evaluator choice. These results indicate that current systems do not yet reliably support long-horizon companion continuity and that audits must distinguish persona enactment, trajectory recall, evaluator provenance, and deployment context rather than collapse them into a single trust or stability score.

</details>


### [102] [Fragility of Value under Imperfect Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28881)
*Winter Cross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对齐问题的数学模型，研究在理想化对齐训练下，代理的价值函数满足代理条件后仍可能因过度优化导致灾难性后果（η-灾难性价值函数）的条件，并建议采用限制优化压力的设计（如quantilizers）而非仅依赖预部署训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统承担更多责任，确保其与人类价值观对齐愈发重要。AI安全中一个常见担忧是人类价值是脆弱的——对不完美的人类价值代理指标过度优化可能导致灾难性后果。本文旨在从理论上分析这种风险何时会发生。

Method: 构建一个对齐问题的数学模型：代理经历理想化对齐训练，保证其价值函数满足代理条件后才开始优化世界。通过数学推导，识别人类价值函数和多种代理条件准确性的条件，在这些条件下具有η-灾难性价值函数（即在优化力极限下保证将人类价值期望降至η以下的代理）会被部署。

Result: 主要结果是识别出了η-灾难性价值函数代理会被部署的充分条件，这些条件涉及人类价值函数的性质以及代理条件的准确性。

Conclusion: 研究结果突显了过度优化的危险性，并建议AI设计应限制优化压力（例如采用quantilizers），而不是仅依赖预部署训练来保证安全。

Abstract: As more responsibility is placed upon AI systems, it becomes increasingly important to guarantee that these systems are aligned with humanity. A common fear in AI safety is that human value is fragile -- that is, optimizing too heavily for an imperfect proxy to human values will lead to a catastrophic outcome. In this paper, we present a model of the alignment problem where an agent undergoes idealized alignment training that guarantees its value function satisfies a proxy condition before optimizing the world. Our primary results identify conditions on the human value function and the accuracy of several proxy conditions under which an agent with an $η$-catastrophic value function, one that is guaranteed to take the expectation of human value below $η$ in the limit of optimizing power, would be deployed. Our results highlight the danger of overoptimization and motivate AI designs that limit optimization pressure, such as quantilizers, rather than relying solely on pre-deployment training.

</details>


### [103] [NeSyFS: A Neuro-symbolic Fast-Slow Thinking Framework for LLM Agent under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28942)
*Duo Xu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号快慢思考框架（NeSyFS），通过知识图谱表示信念状态，结合快速反应与基于扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛的不确定性感知规划，统一解决LLM智能体在部分可观测环境中的信念推断、目标错位和不确定性规划问题，在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自主智能体在部分可观测环境中面临三大挑战：信念状态推断、任务目标错位、不确定性下的规划。现有方法通常基于完整或摘要化的行动-观察历史来决策，但其中冗余和无关信息会误导LLM智能体的决策。

Method: 提出NeSyFS框架：(1) 用知识图谱（KG）表示信念状态，为各模块提供三元组上下文；(2) 快思考模块执行反应式动作；(3) 慢思考模块遵循扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（TSMC）算法的高层结构进行不确定性感知规划；(4) 反思模块纠正快思考动作，并在反应式动作反复失败时切换到慢思考模块。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个代表性基准上的实验表明，该框架相比以往方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: NeSyFS通过整合神经符号推理与快慢思考机制，以统一方式有效应对部分可观测环境中的多挑战，验证了其优于现有方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in applications such as self-reflection, retrieval-augmented generation, and scientific discovery. In these settings, agents must act based on limited observations rather than full environmental states, leading to partial observability. This introduces several key challenges: belief state inference, task objective misalignment, and planning under uncertainty. Prior approaches typically condition actions on full or summarized action-observation histories whose redundant and irrelevant information can mislead the decision making of LLM agent. Inspired by human cognition, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic fast-slow thinking (NeSyFS) framework for LLM agent, addressing the challenges introduced by partial observability in a unified approach. We use a knowledge graph (KG) to represent the belief state, providing triplets as context for every module of NeSyFS. The fast-thinking module performs reactive action, while slow-thinking conducts a new uncertainty-aware planning by following the high-level structure of twisted sequential Monte Carlo (TSMC) algorithm. To mitigate the misalignment of task objective, a reflection module is used to reflect fast-thinking actions, and also switches to the slow-thinking module whenever reactive actions repeatedly fail. Experiments on three representative benchmarks, i.e. ALFWorld, Webshop, and ScienceWorld, demonstrate significant advantages over previous methods.

</details>


### [104] [MerchantBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Long-Term Coherence in E-Commerce Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28956)
*Qiming Shi,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Tianjun Pan,Shaokang Fu,Chengyu Wang,Siyue Li,Yaping Cheng,Di Weng,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MerchantBench，一个基于真实电商数据的365天卖家侧模拟基准，用于评估大语言模型智能体在长时间跨度上保持行为连贯性的能力。实验显示即使最先进的LLM在最终净资产上仅达到人类参与者的27.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准大多聚焦于有即时成功标准的受限任务，而真实部署场景要求智能体具备长期连贯性（Long-Term Coherence）——在长时间跨度上保持目的性行为并基于累积证据调整决策的能力。评估这一能力需要持续环境、行动约束未来选择、异构延迟反馈以及可测量的累积效应，而现有基准无法满足这些要求。

Method: MerchantBench是一个365天的订单级电商模拟环境，基于98,843条真实电商产品记录构建，配备26个工具供智能体交互。环境覆盖四大决策领域：产品采购、上架与定价控制、现金流管理、混合延迟反馈适应。环境耦合了即时可观测的上游供应商事件与延迟的下游订单结果，要求智能体跟踪单个订单生命周期并重新审视早期决策。实验在两种智能体框架下评估8个LLM，共48次运行。

Result: 实验结果显示，即便是最新的LLM与人类参与者之间存在显著差距：最佳LLM配置达到的平均最终净资产仅为人类参与者平均值的27.3%。

Conclusion: MerchantBench揭示了LLM在长期连贯性评估上的显著不足，证明当前最先进的语言模型在需要长期决策一致性、累积证据适应和跨时序决策关联的真实场景中仍远不及人类水平，为未来研究提供了明确的方向和量化基准。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly evaluated as autonomous tool users, yet most benchmarks focus on bounded tasks with immediate success criteria. Real-world deployments often require Long-Term Coherence, the capacity to preserve purposeful behavior across extended horizons while adapting decisions to accumulated evidence. Evaluating this capacity requires a persistent environment in which actions constrain future choices, feedback arrives at heterogeneous delays, and incoherent behavior produces measurable cumulative effects. Seller-side e-commerce provides a suitable setting for this evaluation through recurrent and interdependent decisions over Product Sourcing, Listing and Pricing Control, Cash-Flow Management, and Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation. We introduce MerchantBench, a 365-day order-level simulation grounded in 98,843 real e-commerce product records and equipped with 26 tools for agent interaction. MerchantBench couples promptly observable Upstream Supplier Events with delayed Downstream Order Outcomes, requiring agents to follow individual order lifecycles and revisit earlier decisions. We evaluate eight LLMs under two agent frameworks in 48 runs, each spanning 365 simulated days. Our results reveal a substantial gap between even the latest LLMs and human participants, with the best LLM configuration attaining only 27.3\% of the mean final net assets achieved by human participants.

</details>


### [105] [Scaling Scientific Discovery Environments for Turn-Level Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28990)
*Yucheng Xu,Keyi Zhang,Yuyang Yu,Min Zhang,Shiyuan Meng,Pei Chu,Zhongying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDisco，一个用于训练科学发现智能体的可扩展框架，通过流程可验证环境（SciThèque）、DAG锚定的轨迹合成和DiscoPO优化方法，使14B模型在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在数据驱动的科学发现任务中展现出潜力，但长期科学分析因缺乏基于真实科学数据的流程监督环境而受到限制。

Method: SciDisco框架包含三个核心组件：1) SciThèque——将假设、数据集、隐藏证据图和验证器编译成可在交互过程中检查分析进度的任务环境；2) DAG锚定的轨迹合成——利用这些环境构建经验证器过滤的多轮演示；3) DiscoPO——以环境为训练信号来源，对产生可验证分析证据的动作进行轮次级信用分配。

Result: SciDisco-14B在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上达到了最先进水平（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: 通过流程可验证的环境为科学发现智能体提供训练信号，可以有效提升长时程科学分析能力，且14B规模的模型即可超越现有基准。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown promising capabilities in data-driven scientific discovery tasks, where an agent interacts with an execution environment and produces a statistical claim. Long-horizon scientific analysis remains constrained by the lack of process supervised environments over real-world scientific data. This paper introduces SciDisco, a scalable framework for training Scientific Discovery agents in process-verifiable environments. SciThèque compiles hypotheses, datasets, hidden evidence graphs, and verifiers into task environments where analytical progress can be checked during interaction. DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis uses these environments to construct verifier-filtered multi-turn demonstrations. DiscoPO then uses the environment as the source of training signal, assigning turn-level credit to actions that produce verifiable analytical evidence. Experiments show that SciDisco-14B reaches state-of-the-art on hypothesis-driven scientific data analysis benchmarks.

</details>


### [106] [MMShopBench: A Real-Log Benchmark for Multimodal, Multi-Turn Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29002)
*Zeying Hao,Hao Guo,Mengtao Xu,Yimin Hu,Yuheng Song,Zesheng Zhou,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MMShopBench，这是首个基于真实购物日志的多模态、多轮购物智能体基准测试。它要求智能体从用户图片和多轮对话中联合推断购买意图和强制性产品需求，并通过图像与文本检索产品、验证候选产品是否满足所有需求。作者构建了离线购物沙箱以确保实验可复现，并证明微调开源模型能显著缩小与专有模型的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有购物智能体基准大多依赖纯文本或合成请求，未能充分覆盖真实世界中通过图片和语言联合表达的复杂购物需求，无法准确反映实际应用场景。

Method: MMShopBench基于清洗和人工标注的真实购物日志构建，提供每项请求的购买意图和强制性产品需求的金标准标注。智能体需联合推断用户图片与多轮对话中的需求，通过图像和文本搜索检索候选产品，并利用产品图像和结构化属性验证候选是否满足所有需求。作者还构建了配套训练集用于微调开源模型，并搭建了离线购物沙箱。

Result: 评估了多种代表性开源和专有模型，采用基于证据的多模态评估协议。实验表明，在离线购物沙箱中微调后，开源模型的性能与领先专有模型之间的差距显著缩小，验证了训练数据的有效性。

Conclusion: MMShopBench填补了多模态多轮真实购物智能体基准的空白，证明了高质量训练数据能够有效提升开源模型在复杂真实购物场景中的表现，为购物智能体的研究与评估提供了更贴近实际需求的平台。

Abstract: Online shoppers increasingly turn to AI shopping assistants, using images and multi-turn dialogue to express and refine product needs that are difficult to articulate in text alone. However, existing benchmarks largely rely on text-only or synthetic requests, underrepresenting complex real-world shopping requirements jointly expressed through images and language. We introduce MMShopBench, the first real-log benchmark for multimodal, multi-turn shopping agents. Built from carefully cleaned and manually annotated shopping logs, MMShopBench provides ground-truth annotations of each request's purchase intent and mandatory product requirements. Agents must infer these requirements jointly from user images and multi-turn dialogue, retrieve candidate products through image and text search, and verify that each candidate satisfies all requirements using its product images and structured attributes. We evaluate representative open-source and proprietary models using an evidence-grounded multimodal protocol and construct a companion training set for fine-tuning an open-source model. To ensure reproducible experimentation, we build an offline shopping sandbox, where fine-tuning substantially narrows the performance gap between our open-source model and leading proprietary models, demonstrating the effectiveness of our training data.

</details>


### [107] [Evidence-Grounded Constraint Checking in Construction Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29058)
*Rashid Mushkani,Hugo Berard,Shin Koseki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于证据的专业文档审阅流水线，用于约束检查任务。该流水线对提取的事实进行规范化、以确定性方式执行四态规则、保留源文本跨度并升级未解决案例。在29个建筑项目的160个参考任务上，重复四系统测试显示：将四图像预算从整页概览重新分配为一个概览加三个重叠图块，可将项目家族标准化决策准确率提升10.6个百分点，但该效果在更广泛的测试中未持续存在（Region-RAG变化-4.1点）。精确发现集恢复率较低、误通过仍常见、重复运行一致性校准不良，表明存在分辨率-广度权衡而非区域聚焦证据的普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业文档审阅本质上是一个约束检查问题，其决策依赖于文本、几何、页面和文档修订之间的关系。现有方法缺乏一个能够将证据与规则执行相衔接的可靠管线，尤其是在PDF证据分配（证据选择策略）方面，对于区域聚焦（大图块）与页面广度（整页概览）哪种方式更优存在不确定性。作者希望通过系统化的证据路由和实验评估来回答这一问题。

Method: 提出一个证据支持的四阶段流水线：(1) 规范化提取的事实；(2) 以确定性方式执行四态规则（four-state rules）；(3) 保留源文本跨度（source spans）以实现可追溯性；(4) 将未解决案例升级处理。证据分配通过一个PDF证据分配器完成。实验设计包含两部分：重复四系统测试（从检索到的页面概览重分配四图像预算为一个概览加三个重叠图块）和一个不相交的两系统广度扩展测试，共覆盖29个建筑项目的160个参考任务。

Result: 重复测试中，重分配图像预算为"一个概览+三个重叠图块"使项目家族标准化决策准确率提高10.6个百分点（95%项目聚类bootstrap CI: 4.3-18.0; p=0.031）。但在更广泛的广度扩展测试中该效果未持续：Region-RAG改变准确率-4.1点（95% CI: -10.2至1.9; p=0.209），等图像量敏感性测试反而倾向于页面广度。精确发现集恢复率低、误通过常见、重复运行一致性校准不佳。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了证据选择中的"分辨率-广度权衡"：区域聚焦证据（重叠图块）在特定项目族上显著有益，但在更广泛的场景中并不具有普遍优势。因此，证据路由策略应基于规则感知（rule-aware），而非一律采用区域聚焦或页面广度方案，且仍需专家审阅作为最终保障。

Abstract: Professional-document review is a constraint-checking problem in which decisions depend on relations among text, geometry, pages, and document revisions. We present an evidence-grounded pipeline that normalizes extracted facts, executes four-state rules deterministically, retains source spans, and escalates unresolved cases. We evaluate its PDF evidence allocator on 160 reference-based tasks from 29 construction projects using a repeated four-system test and a disjoint two-system breadth extension. In the repeated test, reallocating a four-image budget from retrieved page overviews to one overview and three overlapping tiles improves project-family standardized decision accuracy by 10.6 percentage points (95% project-cluster bootstrap CI: 4.3 to 18.0; exact p = 0.031). This effect does not persist in the broader block: Region-RAG changes accuracy by -4.1 points (95% CI: -10.2 to 1.9; exact p = 0.209), while an equal-image sensitivity favors page breadth. Exact finding-set recovery remains low, false passes remain common, and repeated-run agreement is poorly calibrated. The results identify a resolution-breadth trade-off rather than a universal advantage for region-focused evidence, motivating rule-aware evidence routing and expert review.

</details>


### [108] [On the Generalization of Steering Vectors for Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29062)
*Matthew Nguyen,Kyle Cox,Austin Meek,Iván Arcuschin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活引导（activation steering）如何提高思维链（CoT）的忠实性（faithfulness），即模型是否在推理中口头承认其使用的关键线索。研究发现：引导仅在最大模型（Gemma-3 12B）上可靠地提高线索承认率，但一旦有效，其效果能跨线索类型和数据集泛化；四种构建引导向量的方法效果相当；引导并未改变线索使用率，而是减少了"隐藏线索使用"（使用了线索但未口头承认的情况）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理是AI安全的重要方向，因为模型将其推理过程言语化后就可以被监控。然而，有时模型不会口头承认其推理中起关键作用的步骤（例如，被暗示错误答案的线索），这种不忠实的CoT会削弱推理监控的有效性。先前工作表明激活引导可以提升CoT忠实性，但对其泛化能力、构建方法的影响以及作用机制尚不清楚，本研究旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 在提示问答场景下，对三个模型（Gemma-3 4B、Qwen-3.5 9B、Gemma-3 12B）进行激活引导实验。系统性地评估引导效果在以下维度上的泛化：（1）跨线索类型；（2）跨数据集；（3）四种不同的引导向量构建方法（包括一种优化目标不涉及特定线索的方法）。此外，通过测量线索使用率和隐藏线索使用率，检验引导是否通过改变线索使用行为来起作用，而非仅仅更改口头表达行为。

Result: （1）仅最大模型Gemma-3 12B上的引导能可靠地提高线索承认率。（2）当引导有效时，其效果能跨线索类型和数据集广泛泛化，效果大小主要取决于评估设置而非向量的训练设置。（3）四种引导向量构建方法的效果相似，包括不依赖特定线索的优化目标。（4）引导未改变线索使用率，但减少了隐藏线索使用（即使用了线索但未承认的情况），表明引导直接作用于口头表达行为而非线索本身的显著性。

Conclusion: 激活引导在提升CoT忠实性方面具有鲁棒性和泛化能力，但其有效性依赖于模型规模。引导的作用机制似乎是提高模型对已使用线索的口头承认，而非改变其线索使用行为本身。这为AI安全中的推理监控提供了实用的技术路径，但也提示小规模模型上引导的效果有限。

Abstract: Model capabilities have improved in large part due to scaling chain of thought. This has been a promising development for AI safety--where models verbalize their reasoning, it is possible to monitor it. However, in some cases, models do not verbalize important steps in their reasoning process. For example, models prompted with a cue suggesting the incorrect answer may fail to acknowledge that cue, even when it appears instrumental to their conclusion. When chain of thought (CoT) fails to disclose instrumental reasoning steps, we describe it as unfaithful. Prior work has shown that activation steering can be a useful method to improve faithfulness in CoT. We extend this line of work by studying how well steering for faithfulness generalizes across cue types, datasets, and methods of constructing the steering vector for three models (Gemma-3 4B, Qwen-3.5 9B, Gemma-3 12B) in a cued question-answering setting. While steering reliably increases cue acknowledgment for only the largest model (Gemma-3 12B), we find that when steering is effective, its effect generalizes broadly across cue types and datasets--in cross-cue and cross-dataset analyses, effect size is determined primarily by the evaluation setting, rather than the vector's train setting. How the vector is built also matters little--four construction methods, including one whose optimization target mentions no specific cue, yield similar effect sizes. Finally, we consider the possibility that steering promotes the salience of the cue and causes greater cue use, rather than targeting verbalization behaviors. However, we find no evidence for this--steering leaves the rate of cue use roughly unchanged while reducing hidden cue use, i.e., cue use that is not acknowledged.

</details>


### [109] [Harnessing the Wisdom of LLM Crowds through Complementarity-Driven Iterative Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29087)
*Yanbin Fang,Xuan Wei,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WILC框架，将大语言模型的集体智能重新概念化为"接力式互补"：通过迭代反思与精炼的工作流，以及基于互补性的双门控模型选择机制，实现多个LLM的顺序协作，以超越任何单一模型的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个LLM存在模型特定的能力局限，而已有方法事先固定模型组合方式，忽略了复杂问题求解中模型互补性的动态、状态依赖特征。需要一种能够根据当前输出瓶颈动态选择后继模型的框架。

Method: WILC框架包含两个设计原则：(1) 迭代反思与精炼（iterative reflection-and-refinement）建立状态保留的工作流，使模型诊断并改进先前输出；(2) 互补性驱动的模型选择通过双门控机制进行：前瞻性互补适配度（PCF）选择最适应当前瓶颈的工作者模型，后验互补增益（PCG）评估所选转换是否改进演化中的解。

Result: 在四个多样化基准上的实验表明，WILC优于单模型自精炼、集成方法和查询路由方法。在标准化定价假设下，WILC以约7倍更低的每查询估计成本达到GPT-5.2的平均基准表现，同时通过自托管部署实现数据主权。

Conclusion: 该研究将智慧众包理论从静态聚合扩展到序列化AI互补，为多AI协调提供了可迁移的设计原则。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in enterprise settings, yet individual models remain bounded by model-specific capability limitations. These heterogeneous boundaries pose a deployment challenge, but also create an opportunity: strategically coordinating multiple LLMs may unlock collective intelligence exceeding any single model. Existing approaches fix how models are combined in advance, overlooking the dynamic, state-dependent role of complementarity in complex problem solving. Drawing on the wisdom-of-crowds paradigm, we reconceptualize collective LLM intelligence as relay-style complementarity: a sequential process in which each successor model is selected to address the specific bottleneck identified in its predecessor's output. To operationalize this, we propose WILC (Wisdom Integration of LLM Crowds), a framework grounded in two design principles. First, iterative reflection-and-refinement establishes a state-preserving workflow through which models diagnose and refine prior outputs. Second, complementarity-driven model selection governs transitions via a dual-gate mechanism: prospective complementarity fit (PCF) identifies the worker most suited to the current bottleneck, while posterior complementarity gain (PCG) evaluates whether the selected transition improves the evolving solution. Experiments across four diverse benchmarks show that WILC outperforms existing approaches, including single-model self-refinement, ensemble methods, and query-routing methods. Under standardized pricing assumptions, WILC matches the average benchmark performance of GPT-5.2 at roughly 7 times lower estimated per-query cost, while facilitating data sovereignty through self-hosted deployment. This study extends wisdom-of-crowds theory from static aggregation to sequential AI complementarity and provides transferable design principles for multi-AI coordination.

</details>


### [110] [CAGE: Certified Authorization under Typed-Return Uncertainty for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29190)
*Blaise Delattre,Cong Wang,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAGE框架，用于对工具使用型LLM代理的权限门控进行鲁棒认证。针对"返回值与源绑定错误+数值漂移"这一联合邻域，CAGE将离散分支枚举与连续扰动认证相结合，证明单独验证分类与数值通道无法组合出安全保证，而联合验证可消除点级门控在预算内产生的假允许。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时权限门控通常只授权观测到的返回值和动作，但未防护返回值在绑定到其来源时可能出现的微小错误。作者发现，分别对分类通道和数值通道进行认证并不能组合得到安全保证——单独在每个通道上安全的扰动，联合起来可能使同一动作变得不安全。因此需要在联合邻域上进行认证。

Method: CAGE直接对"一次可容许的绑定故障加上有界数值漂移"构成的联合邻域进行认证。它将离散分支精确枚举，并在每个分支内对连续扰动进行认证。根据策略是否可执行，提供三种变体：CAGE-Exact直接认证策略本身；CAGE-Lip和CAGE-RS则基于显式、可测量的保真度假设来认证学习到的门控函数。

Result: 在合成数据、策略即代码、监管规则和真实交易等多种场景下，CAGE消除了精确点级门控所允许的预算内假允许（false allows），同时保留了一部分有用比例的决策可自主执行。

Conclusion: CAGE证明了分通道认证的不完备性，并通过联合邻域直接认证的方式提供了更严格的鲁棒权限保证，在减少虚假授权的同时维持了合理的自动化决策比例。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents act on typed tool returns, records pairing provenance and categorical fields with numerical values. Runtime permission gates generally authorize the observed return and action, leaving the decision unprotected against small errors in how the return was bound to its source. We ask whether a candidate action stays authorized over a declared neighborhood of plausible correctly bound returns: one admissible binding fault plus bounded numerical drift. We prove that certifying the categorical and numerical channels separately does not compose: perturbations that are safe on each channel alone can jointly turn the same action unsafe. CAGE certifies this joint neighborhood directly, enumerating the discrete branches exactly and certifying the continuous perturbation within each branch. Across synthetic, policy-as-code, regulatory, and real-transaction settings, CAGE removes the in-budget false allows that accurate pointwise gates admit, while keeping a useful fraction of decisions autonomous. When the policy is executable, CAGE-Exact certifies the policy itself; otherwise CAGE-Lip and CAGE-RS certify a learned gate under an explicit, measured fidelity assumption.

</details>


### [111] [MirrorCraft: Paired Evaluation under Hidden Rule Changes in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29218)
*Jianxin Gao,Beini Hu,Runze Li,Wanli Peng,Ruohan Lei,Jinyuan Zhang,Linna Deng,Tianyi Yu,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MirrorCraft，一个用于评估LLM智能体在Minecraft隐藏规则变化场景下表现的配对基准。每对Vanilla-Mirror世界中仅修改服务器端规则（通过数据包），其余地形、资源、目标等保持一致，通过规则干预效应（RIE）量化规则变化对智能体任务进展的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Minecraft基准评测均基于固定游戏机制，无法检验智能体在熟悉的配方、掉落物等规则发生改变时是否仍能持续取得进展。作者希望构建一个能够评估智能体适应隐藏规则变化能力的可控评测环境。

Method: MirrorCraft包含五个受控生物群系、六套规则套件、三个进展目标、两个模型家族和六种智能体配置（基于共享的Mineflayer接口）。每对Vanilla-Mirror世界中，通过数据包仅修改选定的服务器端规则，其余（地形、出生点、资源放置、目标、界面和行动预算）完全匹配。使用确定性进展里程碑和成功率评估任务进展，并以规则干预效应（RIE）衡量Vanilla与Mirror之间的性能差异。

Result: 实验表明，隐藏规则变化在不同规则套件间的影响差异显著。在未提供规则描述的全部配置中，ReAct实现了最高的镜像世界综合得分。提供精确规则在三个目标上的平均进展和完成率方面带来适度提升。

Conclusion: MirrorCraft将Minecraft评测从固定机制扩展到动态规则变化场景，为研究智能体在当前世界规则与熟悉规则不一致时如何利用游戏交互结果进行适应提供了受控实验设置。

Abstract: With the prosperity of the large language models (LLMs), it has become an interesting topic: how do LLM-based agents work in Minecraft? Unfortunately, most existing benchmarks evaluate them under fixed game mechanics. High performance in these settings does not show whether an agent can continue making progress when familiar recipes, drops, and other rules change. In this paper, we introduce MirrorCraft, a paired benchmark for evaluating agents under hidden rule changes in Minecraft. Each Mirror world is a copy of its paired Vanilla world, with selected server-side rules modified by the corresponding datapack. Terrain, spawn, resource placement, objective, interface, and action budget remain matched within every Vanilla-Mirror pair. MirrorCraft includes five controlled biomes, six rule suites, three progression objectives, two model families, and six agent configurations under a shared Mineflayer interface. We evaluate task progress with deterministic advancement milestones and success rate and use the Rule Intervention Effect (RIE) to measure the performance change between matched Vanilla and Mirror worlds. The experiments show that hidden rule changes have strongly different effects across suites. Among the configurations evaluated without rule descriptions, ReAct achieves the highest pooled Mirror score. Providing the exact rules yields modest gains in average progress and completion across all three objectives. MirrorCraft extends Minecraft evaluation beyond fixed mechanics and provides a controlled setting for studying how agents use gameplay outcomes when the rules of the current world differ from familiar ones.

</details>


### [112] [Don't Mix Rewards, Mix Policies: Policy Decomposition and Optimization for Multi-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29246)
*Ruiming Liang,Yi Zhong,Yizhen Yuan,Yinan Zheng,Tianyi Tan,Tianyue Wang,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Xianyuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一种基于策略空间分解与组合的多奖励强化学习框架，通过优化一组独立的正面策略和一个全局负面策略来缓解多奖励优化中的目标冲突，同时在推理时通过灵活的策略组合实现可控性，在科学推理、工具使用推理和帮助性-安全性对齐任务上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要适应不同的人类价值和用例，因此多奖励强化学习（multi-reward RL）在LLM中变得日益重要。然而，多奖励优化面临更严重的对齐税（alignment tax）问题——不同优化目标之间可能相互权衡甚至冲突，导致训练不稳定且效率低下。

Method: PRISM框架采用策略空间分解与组合的思想：不对多个奖励进行直接组合（compositing），而是优化一组独立的正面（positive）策略和一个全局负面（negative）策略。通过这种方式缓解多奖励策略优化中的冲突，同时在推理阶段通过灵活的策略组合实现可控性。

Result: 在科学推理、工具使用推理和帮助性-安全性对齐三个任务上，PRISM一致性地优于现有的多奖励RL基线方法，并额外提供了推理时的偏好控制能力（inference-time preference control）。

Conclusion: PRISM通过将多奖励问题转化为策略空间中的分解与组合问题，有效缓解了多目标优化中的冲突，提升了训练稳定性与效率，并赋予模型推理时可控性，为多奖励RL在LLM后训练中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are expected not just to answer correctly, but to adapt their behavior to different human values and use cases. As a result, multi-reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become an increasingly important problem for LLMs, where each reward captures a different aspect of desired behavior. However, optimizing with multiple rewards suffers from a more severe alignment tax issue, where different optimization objectives can trade off or even conflict with each other, leading to unstable and inefficient post-training. In this work, we propose PRISM, a new multi-reward RL framework built upon the idea of policy-space decomposition and composition. Instead of compositing different rewards, PRISM optimizes a set of standalone positive policies and a global negative policy. This alleviates the potential conflict during multi-reward policy optimization, while enabling controllability during inference by flexible policy composition. Experiments on scientific reasoning, tool-use reasoning, and helpfulness-safety alignment show that PRISM consistently outperforms existing multi-reward RL baselines, with extra controllability for inference-time preference control.

</details>


### [113] [Tool Specifications Matter: Uncovering and Mitigating Safety Risks in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29254)
*Minghui Pan,Jiayuxuan Yang,Yuanyuan Yuan,Yu Jiang,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现schema格式的工具规范是大语言模型作为AI代理时安全性能下降的主要来源，提出推理时安全防护方法SafeKeep，将安全判断与工具执行解耦，使用扁平化文本工具规范评估请求，保留原始schema格式用于执行。在两项基准测试和四个LLM上，SafeKeep将有害请求的拒答率从23.8%提升至70.6%，将提示注入攻击成功率从25.6%降至2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在部署为AI代理（结合外部工具）时安全性显著下降，但这一退化现象的根源尚未被充分理解。理解并解决代理场景下的安全退化问题，对于安全部署LLM代理系统至关重要。

Method: 首先通过白盒表征分析识别出schema格式的工具规范是安全退化的主要来源——它会削弱模型内部的拒答信号并导致不安全的工具执行。基于此发现，提出SafeKeep，一种推理时防护方法，将安全判断与工具执行解耦：使用扁平化文本形式（而非schema格式）的工具规范来评估请求安全性，同时保留原始schema格式用于实际工具执行。

Result: 在两个代表性基准和四个LLM（包括白盒和黑盒模型）上，SafeKeep将有害请求的平均拒答率从23.8%提升到70.6%，将观察级提示注入的平均攻击成功率从25.6%降至2.5%；优于现有防护方法，且保持任务处理能力。

Conclusion: schema格式的工具规范是代理安全退化的关键成因；通过在推理时解耦安全判断与工具执行，并使用不同格式的工具规范分别处理安全评估和实际执行，可以有效恢复模型的安全拒答能力，同时不损害任务处理性能。相关代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) with external tools, enabling them to perform complex tasks and translate model outputs into consequential real-world actions. Yet LLMs often become substantially less safe when deployed as agents, and the source of this degradation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we identify schema-formatted tool specifications as a primary source of agent safety degradation and show, through white-box representation analysis, that they weaken the model's internal refusal signals and contribute to unsafe tool execution. Building on this finding, we propose SafeKeep, an inference-time safeguard that decouples safety judgment from tool execution: it assesses requests using flattened textual tool specifications while retaining the original schema-formatted specifications for execution. Across two representative benchmarks and four LLMs, including both white-box and black-box models, SafeKeep increases the average refusal rate for harmful requests from 23.8% to 70.6% and reduces the average attack success rate under observation-level prompt injection from 25.6% to 2.5%. It also outperforms existing safeguards and preserves task-handling capability. We release the code and data at https://github.com/snowcatsmoking/SafeKeep .

</details>


### [114] [MAGA: Multi-Platform Self-Fusion of GUI Agents via Structured Action Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29320)
*Hang Yan,Zhangxuan GU,Beitong Zhou,Jiaxuan Chen,Runze Li,Yusong Hu,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGA是一种面向跨环境GUI智能体的动作感知权重聚合方法，通过根据结构化动作的正确性重新分配蒸馏训练信号，抑制无效或错误的监督，仅聚焦于错误动作的学习，同时引入训练时的提示（hint）优化教师监督信号，在8B和更大规模模型上均达到最高平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体通常是领域特定的（如仅适用于移动、网页或桌面环境），限制了部署和用户体验。虽然权重合并可以将领域专家模型直接合并为单一跨环境策略，但在专家意见分歧时会破坏可执行动作；而在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽避免了冲突的教师监督，却将所有响应标记（token）等同对待，忽略了动作令牌是环境与智能体之间唯一交互接口这一关键事实。

Method: MAGA采用动作感知的训练信号重分配机制：基于生成动作的正确性，抑制不必要或无效的蒸馏信号，并聚焦于错误动作的学习。此外，引入训练专用的提示（training-only hint）来优化领域特定教师提供的监督信号，且不改变学生模型的输入。

Result: 在两种模型规模上，MAGA均取得最高平均成功率：在8B规模上超过最强基线2.0%，并达到与教师模型几乎相同的平均性能。

Conclusion: MAGA通过动作感知的蒸馏信号分配策略有效解决了跨环境GUI智能体训练中权重合并与在线策略蒸馏各自的缺陷，实现了单一模型跨移动、网页和桌面环境的统一策略，且性能接近多个领域特定教师模型的平均水平。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents based on large language models are increasingly deployed across mobile, web, and desktop environments. However, existing agents are typically domain-specific, limiting the deployment and user experience. This motivates the consolidation of specialized models into a single cross-environment policy. Weight merging directly merges domain-specific experts but can corrupt executable actions under expert disagreement, while on-policy distillation (OPD) avoids conflicting teacher supervision yet still treats all response tokens equally during distillation, ignoring that action tokens are the only interface between the environment and the agent. To address this, We introduce MAGA that re-allocates training signal according to the structured action. Based on the correctness of the generated action, it suppresses unnecessary or invalid distillation signals and focuses learning on erroneous actions. Besides, a training-only hint optimizes the supervision signal provided by domain-specific teachers without changing the student input. Across two model scales, MAGA achieves the highest mean success rate, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.0% at 8B and achieves almost the same average performance with teachers.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Component Testing: Validating Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29405)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Luca D'Agati,Giuseppe Tricomi,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Antonio Puliafito,Giovanni Merlino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了257篇论文，提出一个五维分类框架（行为、安全、时间、监管、多智能体）来刻画智能体AI系统的验证问题，指出行为评估相对成熟，而时间有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和开放多智能体系统保障仍不足，并提出了以生命周期为导向的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统通过多步轨迹运作（结合规划、工具使用、记忆、交互和适应），其可接受行为取决于决策如何随时间及环境变化展开，这使得传统组件测试和一次性输入输出评估不再适用，呼唤系统性的验证方法论。

Method: 系统综述了257篇论文，覆盖智能体评估、软件保证、信息物理系统、运行时监控和监管指南等领域；围绕五维分类（行为、安全、时间、监管、多智能体）组织分析，并通过三个跨领域案例（医疗护理、工业运营、智能移动系统）进行实例说明。

Result: 分析显示行为评估相对成熟，但时间有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和开放多智能体系统保障仍发展不足；三个安全关键领域案例展示了五维分类的普遍适用性及文献中记录的实际失败模式。

Conclusion: 智能体AI的可信部署取决于在上下文中验证轨迹而不仅仅是评估孤立的组件；未来研究应聚焦有界自主规范、对抗性轨迹生成、运行时监控和审计就绪的证据结构。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems act through multi-step trajectories that combine planning, tool use, memory, interaction, and adaptation. This behavior stretches validation practice beyond component testing and one-shot input--output evaluation, because acceptable system behavior now depends on how decisions unfold over time and under changing environmental conditions. This survey synthesizes 257 papers spanning agent evaluation, software assurance, cyber-physical systems, runtime monitoring, and regulatory guidance in order to characterize the validation problem for agentic systems. The review is organized around a five-dimension taxonomy covering behavioral, safety, temporal, regulatory, and multi-agent concerns, and uses that taxonomy to map current approaches and expose recurrent coverage gaps. The analysis shows that behavioral evaluation is comparatively mature, while temporal validity, runtime evidence maintenance, regulatory legibility, and open-ended multi-agent systems assurance remain under-developed. Three cross-domain case studies (medical care, industrial operations, smart-mobility systems) provide operational illustrations of how the five taxonomy dimensions recur in safety-critical settings, grounded in the failure patterns documented in the reviewed literature. The paper concludes with a lifecycle-oriented research agenda centered on bounded-autonomy specifications, adversarial trajectory generation, runtime monitoring, and audit-ready evidence structures. The central claim is that trustworthy deployment of agentic AI depends on validating trajectories in context rather than assessing isolated components alone.

</details>


### [116] [ModelEquivBench: Certifying Multi-Relational Evaluation of LLM-Generated Optimization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29431)
*Penglin Zhu,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ModelEquivBench，一个可认证的、多关系评估系统，用于细粒度评估LLM从自然语言生成的优化模型。该系统报告E0-E6七种语义关系的逐对匹配档案，每种判定都附带可独立复核的证据（可重放追踪、精确有理数证书或反例见证）。在173个基础问题上评估三个模型快照（GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Qwen3.5-397B-A17B），发现不同模型在档案的不同阶段失败，无法压缩为单一准确率分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成优化模型的评估方法要么将生成模型与真实标签简化为等价/不等价的单一判定，要么使用执行成功率，这些标签既无法独立检验，也不能忠实反映两个公式在多个不同语义维度上的一致程度。需要一种更细粒度、可认证的多关系评估体系。

Method: 提出ModelEquivBench，定义E0-E6七级语义关系档案：模型构造与精确输入（E0）、已验证的表示对齐（E1）、同空间与投影可行集关系（E2、E3）、目标函数阶等价（E4）、最优值相等（E5）、最优解集等价（E6）。每种判定附带关系适配的、可独立复核的证据：E0-E1用可重放追踪或显式映射，正面的E2-E6用精确有理数证书，负面的用显式见证。不完整的映射搜索、不支持的结构和资源限制产生类型化的UNKNOWN或N/A结果，未满足的先决条件报告为ABSENT。

Result: 在173个基础问题（每模型346个单元）、无修复协议下评估三个模型快照：49、35和25个单元包含可执行候选但在至少一个支持关系上被认证为负面；25、8和18个结构拒绝发生在E2在已验证映射下认证了可行集等价的对比对上。三个模型在不同阶段失败，无法被有意义地压缩为单一准确率分数。

Conclusion: ModelEquivBench证明了优化模型生成的评估需要多关系、可认证的细粒度分析，而不是粗粒度等价判定。不同模型在档案的不同阶段暴露不同缺陷，这种区分是单一准确率指标无法体现的，表明模型评估需要更丰富的语义剖面。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate optimization models from natural language, but existing evaluation often reduces a generated model and its ground truth to a single equivalent/not-equivalent verdict or an execution-success rate--labels that are neither independently checkable nor faithful to the multiple distinct senses in which two formulations can agree. We present ModelEquivBench, a certifying, multi-relational evaluation system that reports a per-pair semantic profile E0--E6: model construction and exact ingestion (E0), verified representation alignment (E1), same-space and projected feasible-set relations (E2, E3), objective-order equivalence (E4), optimal-value equality (E5), and optimizer-set equivalence (E6). Each decided entry carries relation-appropriate, independently re-checkable evidence: replayable traces or explicit maps for E0--E1, exact-rational certificates for positive E2--E6 conclusions, and explicit witnesses for supported negatives. Incomplete mapping search, unsupported structure, and resource limits produce typed UNKNOWN or N/A outcomes rather than guesses, while unmet prerequisites are reported as ABSENT. Using ModelEquivBench to evaluate three model snapshots--GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Qwen3.5-397B-A17B--on the same frozen cohort of 173 base problems (346 cells per model) under a no-repair protocol, the resulting profiles expose distinctions that coarse baselines do not represent: 49, 35, and 25 cells contain executable candidates that are nevertheless certified negative on at least one supported relation, and 25, 8, and 18 structural rejections occur on pairs for which E2 certifies mapped feasible-set equality under a verified map. The three model snapshots fail at different stages of the profile and therefore cannot be meaningfully reduced to a single accuracy score.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Retrieval: Analytic Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29440)
*Zhoujin Tian,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yakun Li,Lei Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaMM框架，将长期多模态记忆从单纯的检索记忆扩展到"分析记忆"（analytic memory），通过从对话、图像和元数据中提取带溯源链接的属性值观察，自动发现重复出现的字段结构并物化以供查询，同时利用记忆感知规划器将查询分解为检索和分析操作。在两个长期多模态记忆基准（MemEye和MemGallery）上分别提升11.3%和7.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期多模态记忆系统主要强调检索记忆（retrieval memory），通过摘要和索引组织交互历史以返回查询相关信息的多个粒度，但缺乏对跨交互累积观测的计算能力（如过滤、聚合、排序和时间比较）。需要一种互补的记忆抽象来支持分析性计算需求。

Method: 1) 提出"分析记忆"（analytic memory）形式化概念，将重复出现的多模态观测组织为可查询结构，支持过滤、聚合、排序和时间比较。2) 构建AdaMM框架：从对话、图像和上下文元数据中提取带溯源（provenance）链接的属性值观测，自动发现重复字段结构并物化。3) 推理时使用记忆感知规划器将用户查询分解为检索和分析操作，并将各操作路由到合适的工具。

Result: 在两个长期多模态记忆基准MemEye和MemGallery上，AdaMM分别将性能提升了11.3%和7.3%。

Conclusion: AdaMM证明检索记忆与分析记忆可以在统一框架中互补共存，为长期多模态AI记忆系统提供了超越信息检索的分析性计算能力，为更复杂的长期交互智能奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: Long-term multimodal memory must support not only retrieving relevant information but also computing over observations accumulated across interactions. Existing systems largely emphasize \emph{retrieval memory}, organizing interaction histories through summaries and indexes to return query-relevant information at multiple granularities, from high-level abstractions to underlying records. In this paper, we formulate \emph{analytic memory} as a complementary abstraction that organizes recurring multimodal observations into queryable structures supporting filtering, aggregation, ranking, and temporal comparison. We present AdaMM, a framework that jointly supports retrieval and analytic memory. Rather than relying on application-defined schemas, AdaMM extracts provenance-linked attribute-value observations from dialogue, images, and contextual metadata, discovers recurring field structures, and materializes them for analytical access. At inference time, a memory-aware planner decomposes queries into retrieval and analytic operations and routes each operation to the appropriate tools. Experiments on two long-term multimodal memory benchmarks, MemEye and MemGallery, show that AdaMM improves performance by up to 11.3\% and 7.3\%, respectively.

</details>


### [118] [Self-Play Meets Skill Evolution: Self-Evolving Search Agents that Pose, Solve, and Remember](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29468)
*Zenghuang Fu,Zhaoyang Li,Qiuyuan Ai,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Ante Wang,Guannan He,Changwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SESA（自进化技能增强代理）框架，使程序性记忆成为工具增强搜索自博弈中的演化状态。通过挑战者生成问题、求解者检索技能、将失败提炼为技能写回记忆的闭环，实现任务生成与技能记忆的共同进化。在七个开放域和多跳问答基准上，SESA相比SSP平均提升1.2-3.2个点，相比SkillRL提升0.9个点，并支持无记忆部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自博弈代理生成训练问题时缺乏持久状态——失败仅影响梯度而不显式塑造未来训练分布；外部技能记忆虽保留程序性经验，但通常从固定任务分布中学习。这导致任务生成与技能积累无法协同演化，限制了自博弈训练效率与泛化能力。

Method: SESA采用双参数架构：挑战者（challenger）负责提出训练问题，独立参数化的求解者（solver）单独检索技能。训练过程中，信息性失败被蒸馏为可复用技能写回外部记忆库；更新后的记忆改变求解者行为与成功率，进而改变挑战者的奖励与未来问题分布，形成双向闭环使任务生成和技能记忆共同演化。检索得到的技能同时影响on-policy训练轨迹，其收益既可进入模型参数（支持无记忆部署），也可保留在外部记忆库中（支持推理时检索）。

Result: 在七个开放域和多跳问答基准上，SESA相比SSP在多个骨干模型上平均提升1.2-3.2个准确率点；在统一评估协议下超越技能增强基线SkillRL 0.9个点。对于Qwen3模型，SESA-Off（离线部署）相比SSP保留1.8-2.2个点的提升，最终技能记忆库额外提供0.5-1.0个点的增益。

Conclusion: 演化技能记忆不仅是推理时的即插即用模块，它同时改变策略学习和未来训练分布，并作为可选外部记忆保留额外价值。SESA验证了自博弈中生成与记忆协同演化的有效性。

Abstract: Self-play agents can generate training problems without questions from target benchmarks, but their curricula lack persistent state: failures affect gradients yet do not explicitly shape future practice. External skill memories preserve procedural experience but are typically learned from fixed task distributions. We introduce \textbf{SESA} (Self-Evolving Skill-Augmented Agent), which makes procedural memory an evolving state of tool-augmented search self-play. A challenger poses problems, while a separately parameterized solver alone retrieves skills. Informative failures are distilled into reusable skills and written back to memory. The updated memory changes solver behavior and success, which changes the challenger's reward and the distribution of future problems; the resulting frontier produces new failures that rewrite memory. This bidirectional loop makes task generation and skill memory co-evolve. Because retrieved skills shape on-policy training trajectories, their benefits can enter the model parameters as well as remain in the external bank, enabling memory-free deployment and optional inference-time retrieval. Across seven open-domain and multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, SESA improves average accuracy over SSP by 1.2--3.2 points across multiple backbones and surpasses the skill-augmented SkillRL baseline by 0.9 points under a unified evaluation protocol. On Qwen3 models, SESA-Off retains 1.8--2.2 points of improvement over SSP, while the final skill bank adds a further 0.5--1.0 points. These results show that evolving skill memory is not merely an inference-time plug-in: it changes policy learning and the future training distribution while retaining value as optional external memory. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zenghuang-Fu/SESA-Self-Evolving-Search-Agents.

</details>


### [119] [AMTFV: Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification for LLM Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29549)
*Rui Zou,Yutao Zhu,Mengqi Wei,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AMTFV（Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification），一种通过引入数学工具流（MTF）接口将验证建模与具体执行解耦的大语言模型数学答案验证方法。AMTFV让验证智能体先构建验证工作流，将数学对象和计算意图编码为MTF请求交由工具智能体解析并调度后端进行精确计算，输出结果用于答案判定、修订和验证工作流修订。在DeepSeek、GPT、Gemini三个系列的七个模型配置下、五个数学推理数据集上，AMTFV整体优于基线方法，在单模型配置下较最强基线平均准确率最高提升8.3个百分点，且在中高验证复杂度样本上增益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型数学答案验证方法存在两类局限：一类通过自然语言反思修订输出，难以可靠支持精确计算；另一类直接生成验证程序，过早地将数学建模与底层实现耦合。因此需要一种既支持精确计算又能解耦验证建模与具体执行的方法。

Method: AMTFV引入数学工具流（MTF）作为"中断-执行-恢复"接口，将验证过程解耦为两层：验证智能体负责构建验证工作流，将需要可靠执行的数学对象和计算意图编码为MTF请求；数学工具智能体负责解析请求、生成可执行调用并调度后端进行精确计算。工具输出随后用于候选答案判定、答案修订以及验证工作流修订三个环节。

Result: 在五个数学推理数据集和七种模型配置（DeepSeek、GPT、Gemini）上的实验表明，AMTFV整体优于所评估的代表性基线方法；在单模型配置下，较最强基线平均准确率最高提升8.3个百分点，且在中高验证复杂度样本上提升幅度更大。

Conclusion: 通过MTF接口将验证建模与具体执行解耦，并借助数学工具箱支持精确计算，AMTFV有效提升了LLM数学答案验证的可靠性，尤其对验证复杂度较高的样本具有显著优势。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated strong mathematical problem-solving capabilities, yet reliably verifying their candidate answers remains challenging. Existing representative methods mainly revise outputs through natural-language reflection or assist verification by directly generating verification programs; the former may not reliably support exact computation, whereas the latter prematurely couples mathematical modeling with low-level implementation. We propose AMTFV (Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification). By introducing Mathematical Tool Flow (MTF) as an interrupt--execute--resume interface, AMTFV decouples verification modeling from concrete execution and supports exact computation through a mathematical toolbox. Specifically, the verification agent first constructs a verification workflow, encodes the mathematical objects and computational intent requiring reliable execution in an MTF request, and sends it to the mathematical toolbox agent. The latter parses the request, generates executable calls, and dispatches them to the backend for exact computation. Tool outputs then support candidate-answer adjudication, answer revision, and verification-workflow revision. We evaluate AMTFV on five challenging mathematical reasoning datasets with seven model configurations from DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini. Experimental results show that AMTFV outperforms the representative baselines evaluated in this study overall; under an individual model configuration, it improves average accuracy over the strongest baseline by up to 8.3 percentage points, with larger gains on samples of medium and high verification complexity.

</details>


### [120] [COntExt: Towards Context-Aware Ontology Extension from Operational Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29553)
*Hussain Hussain,Stefan Schöberl,Angelika Schneider,Verena Geist*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COntExt框架，利用结构化运维指标定义作为知识源，自动建议如何将指标中引用的概念和属性集成到现有本体中，实现上下文感知的本体扩展。实验在四个网络安全本体上进行，结果表明指标上下文能提升关系类型预测和数据属性分配的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织以机器可读格式定义运维指标，这些指标定义隐式编码了超出正式本体范围的领域知识，但连接运维指标目录与本体知识目前是手工、临时且劳动密集的。

Method: COntExt框架将本体扩展问题分解为三个子任务：父类预测、关系类型预测和数据属性分配；利用指标上下文（metric-derived context）与本体上下文进行对比，在不同网络安全本体上评估多种算法。

Result: 在四个网络安全本体的实验中，指标上下文在关系类型预测和数据属性分配任务上优于本体上下文基线。

Conclusion: 运维指标目录是实用且未被充分利用的本体扩展来源，该框架能显著降低组织维护本体的成本。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly define operational metrics in structured, machine-readable formats to monitor systems, processes, and compliance. These metric definitions implicitly encode domain knowledge, such as referencing concepts, properties, and relationships, that often extends what is captured in formal ontologies. Yet the connection between operational metric catalogues and ontological knowledge remains manual, ad-hoc, and labor-intensive. We present COntExt, a framework for context-aware ontology extension that takes structured metric definitions as input and suggests how referenced concepts and properties should be integrated into an existing ontology, utilizing the context of these metrics. The framework defines the extension problem as three sub-tasks: parent class prediction, relation type prediction, and data property assignment. Across four cybersecurity ontologies, we evaluate different algorithms for each task. Our results show that metric-derived context improves the suggestions over ontology-context baselines for relation type prediction and data property assignment. Our work demonstrates that operational metric catalogues are a practical and underexploited source for ontology extension. This work enables organizations to maintain their ontologies at a significantly lower cost than manual engineering.

</details>


### [121] [LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning from Preference Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29559)
*Manith Adikari,Bei Peng,Samuele Vinanzi,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LEMUR框架，将偏好强化学习（PbRL）扩展到多目标强化学习（MORL）场景中，通过从多位人类偏好反馈中联合学习多个目标特定的奖励模型和策略，从而在没有预定义奖励函数的情况下解决多目标决策任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习依赖单一标量奖励函数，但现实任务往往涉及多个竞争目标，而真实奖励函数难以定义或不可获得。现有MORL方法假设每个目标都有明确定义的奖励函数，仍面临奖励指定难的问题；偏好强化学习（PbRL）虽能从人类反馈中学习奖励，但主要在单目标设置中研究。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，将偏好反馈引入多目标RL。

Method: LEMUR框架的核心是：1）从多位人类偏好反馈中为每个目标学习独立的奖励模型；2）联合学习多个目标特定的奖励模型和最优策略；3）通过交互式学习让智能体在不同竞争目标之间进行有效平衡，从而在无预定义奖励函数的情况下学习最优多目标策略。

Result: 在多种多目标基准任务上进行了评估，实验结果证明LEMUR在性能上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: LEMUR为无预定义奖励函数的多目标决策任务提供了一个有前景的解决方案，展示了偏好反馈在多目标强化学习中的有效性，为未来多目标决策问题开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems are typically trained using a single, well-specified scalar reward function. However, real-world decision-making tasks often involve multiple, competing objectives, such as performance versus efficiency, where ground-truth reward functions are difficult to specify or inaccessible. While Multi-Objective RL (MORL) addresses such trade-offs by modeling rewards as vectors, existing approaches typically assume access to a well-specified reward function for each objective, inheriting the same challenges faced by single-objective RL. Meanwhile, Preference-based RL (PbRL) has shown great potential in solving complex tasks without access to a pre-defined reward function through reward learning from human feedback, yet has largely been studied in single-objective settings. In this work, we bridge this gap with LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Preference feedback, a novel framework where an agent interactively learns from the preferences of multiple humans to learn optimal multi-objective policies. Our approach jointly learns policies and multiple objective-specific reward models from human feedback, enabling agents to effectively balance competing objectives during learning. We evaluate LEMUR on a variety of benchmark multi-objective tasks, and empirical results demonstrate its superior performance over baseline methods. Our method presents a promising direction for solving multi-objective decision-making tasks without pre-defined reward functions.

</details>


### [122] [DungeonBench: A Benchmark for Rules-Rich Tactical Reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29577)
*Ismayil Ismayilov,Atakan Kara,Kaan Oktay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DungeonBench是一个基于《龙与地下城》战斗场景的战术推理基准测试平台，覆盖2014年SRD中大部分可直接模拟的规则内容，包含Encounter和Day两个轨道，分别评测单场战斗的局部战术决策和跨战斗的资源管理与长期生存权衡。实验表明前沿语言模型虽能赢得直接战斗，但在资源预算、休息时机和规则感知纪律上仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏基准套件大多未充分测试规则密集型的战术推理能力，即在几何、时机、资源、目标和规则交互同时起作用时如何做出良好决策。平滑化的战斗模拟器往往忽略了真实规则系统的复杂性。

Method: 构建覆盖2014 SRD大部分可由模拟器解析的战斗相关规则内容的DungeonBench。在每个步骤提供完整战术观测、待决策事项和索引化的可执行选项列表（包括移动、攻击、法术、反应、目标、准备和稀缺资源）。设计两个评测轨道：Encounter（单场战斗局部战术）和Day（通过持久HP、法术位、消耗品、准备和短休时机串联多场战斗），并采用统一的引擎生成决策流以支持多种策略类型。

Result: 对前沿语言模型策略进行了评测。结果表明完整的战术观测并未使基准饱和：前沿策略通常能赢得直接遭遇战，但在串联遭遇战中暴露了资源预算、休息时机以及规则感知战术纪律方面的失败。

Conclusion: DungeonBench揭示了当前前沿语言模型在规则密集型战术推理上的局限，尤其是在需要跨战斗权衡即时战术优势与未来生存能力的场景中表现不佳，表明该基准为评估和改进此类决策能力提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Games and simulators make valuable benchmarks by turning decisions into measurable outcomes, but many current suites under-test rules-rich tactical reasoning: the ability to choose well when geometry, timing, resources, objectives, and rule interactions all matter at once. We introduce DungeonBench, a benchmark for tactical reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons combat, built to cover the vast majority of combat-relevant 2014 System Reference Document content whose effects can be resolved by the simulator while retaining mechanics that simplified combat simulators often abstract away. At each step, DungeonBench exposes a complete tactical observation, a pending decision, and an indexed list of executable options spanning movement, attacks, spells, reactions, objectives, preparation, and scarce resources. The task is to value legal choices whose consequences depend on action economy, creature traits, battlefield geometry, timing windows, and future encounters. DungeonBench has two tracks: Encounter, which evaluates local tactical play in single fights, and Day, which links encounters through persistent hit points, spell slots, consumables, preparation, and short-rest timing, forcing policies to trade off immediate tactical advantage against future survivability. The same engine-generated decision stream supports heuristic controllers, language-model policies, learned option rankers, and masked-action reinforcement-learning agents. We evaluate frontier language-model policies on this shared decision stream. Results show that full tactical observations do not saturate the benchmark: frontier policies often win direct encounters, but linked encounter days expose failures in resource budgeting, rest timing, and rule-aware tactical discipline.

</details>


### [123] [AgentHPOBench: A Benchmark For Evaluating LLM Agents as Sequential Hyperparameter Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29626)
*Tianyu Huai,Tingshuo Fan,Xinchi Chen,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Shuang Chen,Jie Zhou,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgentHPOBench，一个包含30个可执行机器学习任务的顺序基准测试，用于评估LLM代理作为自主科学智能体时的实验优化能力。实验表明现有代理具备一定的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代改进、复杂日志诊断和达成参考性能方面仍有明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注静态代码生成、论文复现或最终答案正确性，没有直接评估智能体是否能够解读实验证据并据此指导后续超参数决策。随着LLM从代码补全系统演变为自主科学智能体，这一评估能力变得日益重要。

Method: 构建AgentHPOBench顺序基准，包含7个研究类别的30个可执行机器学习任务。每个任务从已验证的基线运行开始，智能体执行多次顺序干预，每一步观察累积的配置、指标和日志后提出下一个有效配置。在统一协议下评估12种广泛使用的智能体和传统HPO基线。

Result: 当前智能体在多个领域展现出可测量的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代改进、复杂日志诊断以及向报告参考性能的持续进展方面仍存在明显局限。

Conclusion: 现有LLM代理虽能进行基本的超参数优化，但距真正自主科学智能体的要求仍有差距，尤其是在长期迭代优化的稳定性和日志理解能力上需要进一步改进。

Abstract: As LLMs evolve from code completion systems into autonomous scientific agents, evaluating their ability to conduct experiments has become increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically focus on static code generation, paper replication, or final answer correctness, but do not directly assess whether agents can interpret experimental evidence and use it to guide subsequent hyperparameter decisions. To address this gap, we introduce AgentHPOBench, a sequential benchmark comprising 30 executable machine learning tasks across seven research categories. Each task begins with a validated baseline run, after which an agent performs several sequential interventions. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated configurations, metrics, and logs before proposing the next valid configuration. We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

</details>


### [124] [Development of FDD-ON: an Ontology for VAV HVAC System Fault Detection and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29657)
*Yimin Chen,Brian Fricke,Bo Shen,Jamie Lian,Mingkan Zhang,James Lo,Yun Zhang,Shi Ye,Jiajing Huang,Han Hu,Chujie Lu,Rui Tang,George Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FDD-ON本体，一个模块化、可扩展的本体，用于正式表示VAV HVAC系统中的组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及相关属性。FDD-ON整合了系统FDD语义，提供了故障、症状和影响库，并通过明确的因果-故障-症状-影响关系支持机器可解释的查询和互操作的FDD应用开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDD技术在HVAC系统可靠性、能效和维护方面至关重要，但实际部署面临数据异构、设备类型多样和诊断输出分散等挑战。FDD领域缺乏数据可解释性和互操作性，导致信息孤岛问题，阻碍了FDD解决方案以及数字孪生FDD框架和AI驱动维护决策系统等应用的实施。

Method: 提出了FDD-ON，一种模块化可扩展本体，用于形式化表示VAV HVAC系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及属性。通过整合FDD语义并提供受控词汇表，构建了完整的故障、症状和影响库，并明确定义了原因-故障-症状-影响关系链。

Result: 使用公开可用的VAV HVAC系统数据集对FDD-ON进行了评估，并通过FDD开发应用进行了展示。结果表明FDD-ON为跨应用的FDD解决方案提供了基础语义框架，支持可扩展、透明和互操作的FDD系统。

Conclusion: FDD-ON为VAV HVAC系统的故障检测与诊断提供了标准化的语义基础，有效解决了领域内数据互操作性和可解释性问题，可作为构建数字孪生FDD框架和AI驱动维护决策系统的底层语义支撑。

Abstract: Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) technology is essential for improving HVAC system reliability, energy efficiency, and maintenance effectiveness. However, effective deployment of FDD solutions in buildings requires structured domain knowledge that can bridge heterogeneous data sources, diverse equipment types, and varied diagnostic outputs. Limited data interpretability and interoperability within the FDD domain have led to fragmented information silos, hindering the implementation of FDD and related applications, such as the digital twin-enabled FDD frameworks and artificial intelligence (AI)-driven maintenance decision-making systems. This paper presents an FDD Ontology (FDD-ON), a modular and extensible ontology to formally represent variable air volume (VAV) HVAC system components, fault types, symptom statuses, fault impacts and associated attributes. FDD-ON integrates HVAC system FDD semantics to provide comprehensive representations of fault and symptom attributes, supported by the well-defined controlled vocabulary. Additionally, FDD-ON offers comprehensive fault, symptom, and impact libraries to capture a broad spectrum of operational abnormalities and their consequences in VAV HVAC systems. Through explicit contributing cause-fault-symptom-impact relations, FDD-ON serves as a machine-interpretable basis for querying diagnostic knowledge, mapping heterogeneous FDD outputs, and developing interoperable FDD-related applications. FDD-ON is evaluated using publicly available VAV HVAC system datasets and demonstrated through FDD development applications. Results indicate that FDD-ON provides a foundational semantic framework for advancing scalable, transparent, and interoperable FDD solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [125] [ExtractBench: A Benchmark for Schema-Guided Enterprise Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29677)
*Boyang Zhang,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Zhaoqi Li,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExtractBench，一个用于 schema 引导提取（schema-guided extraction）的基准测试，也是首个同时评估值准确率、记录完整性（规模化）、grounding 溯源和成本四个维度的基准。包含 4,869 页、370 份企业文档、8 个业务领域和 67 种文档类型，评测发现商业 VLM 在短文档上表现良好但在长文档上会截断记录列表，而编码代理（coding agents）准确率更高但成本更高，LlamaExtract Agentic Plus 在三个指标上均排名第一且成本仅为编码代理的一小部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工作流日益依赖代理进行 schema 引导提取，即给定文档和用户定义的 schema，代理需忠实遵循 schema 产出正确输出并附带源证据作为 grounding 元数据。然而现有基准缺乏对值准确率、大规模记录完整性、grounding 溯源和成本这四方面的综合评价。

Method: 构建 ExtractBench 评测系统，包含 4,869 页/370 份企业文档/8 个业务领域/67 种文档类型，并带有挑战场景标签。采用可扩展的 schema 和 ground-truth 标注流水线，结合独立系统一致性（真实文档）、已知值（合成列表）和人工验证（表格）三种方式。评测采用顺序无关的值 F1（value accuracy）以及词级和页面级 F1 两种 grounding 指标。

Result: 商业 VLM 在短文档上表现良好，但在长文档上经常截断记录列表；编码代理在准确率上更高但成本显著更高。LlamaExtract Agentic Plus 在三个指标（值准确率、grounding、成本效率）上均排名第一，其准确率与编码代理相当但成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: ExtractBench 为 schema 引导提取提供了首个综合多维度的基准，揭示了长文档场景下 VLM 截断问题与编码代理高成本之间的权衡，并展示了在精度和成本之间取得更优平衡的可行方案。

Abstract: Enterprise workflows increasingly rely on agents for \emph{schema-guided extraction}: given a document and a user-defined schema, the agent faithfully follows the schema to produce the correct output with source evidence as grounding metadata. We present ExtractBench, a benchmark for schema-guided extraction and, to our knowledge, the first to score value accuracy, record completeness at scale, grounding, and measured cost together. The evaluation system contains 4,869 pages across 370 enterprise documents, 8 business domains, and 67 document types, with clear tags differentiating their challenge scenarios. The scalable schema and ground-truth curation pipeline combines independent-system agreement for real documents, known values for synthetic lists, and human verification for forms. We report order-insensitive value F1 for value accuracy, plus two grounding metrics for source traceability: word- and page-level F1. Commercial VLMs perform well on short documents but often truncate record lists on long ones, while coding agents retain higher accuracy at much higher cost. LlamaExtract Agentic Plus ranks first on all three metrics, with accuracy comparable to coding agents at a fraction of the cost. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ExtractBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ExtractBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [126] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR)，一种无外部反馈的多轮强化学习框架，通过让模型在每一轮输出解决方案的同时给出正确性判定和置信度分数，将自验证作为自适应计算分配的控制策略，在推理时动态决定是保留答案还是继续细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展测试时计算可以提升推理能力，但统一预算在简单输入上浪费计算，而基于验证器的细化依赖外部反馈。作者希望消除对验证器的依赖，让模型自身学会何时停止、何时继续，从而实现自适应测试时计算分配。

Method: SVR采用基于GRPO的无外部验证器的多轮强化学习。每轮模型输出解决方案、离散正确性判定和置信度分数；只有当判定为Correct且置信度超过阈值时才保留当前答案，否则基于自身自验证继续细化。真实正确性仅用于构建训练奖励（奖励包括解决方案正确性、校准感知的自验证和可停止的正确状态），推理时仅激活自适应停止机制。训练采用固定horizon轨迹，GRPO优化。

Result: 在7个数学推理基准上使用Qwen3.5-2B，SVR达到宏观平均准确率0.563，平均仅需2.99轮推理；在完整的系统对比中超越标准GRPO、强多轮基线和固定预算的oracle引导分数反馈参考方法，同时比固定10轮推理消耗更少轮次。

Conclusion: 学习得到的自验证可以作为有效的内部控制信号，用于答案保留和自适应测试时计算分配，无需外部验证器反馈。

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Global and local error bounds: characterizations via directional derivatives and tangent cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29004)
*Yu Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一般函数（不要求凸性或线性结构）建立了全局和局部误差界的新的刻画方法，利用方向导数和切锥概念，推导了误差界与摄动稳定性、子水平集Hausdorff稳定性等的等价条件，并给出了精确的最优常数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的误差界刻画通常依赖于凸性假设或线性结构，如何在不施加这些限制的情况下，为一般非凸、非光滑函数建立系统的误差界刻画是一个重要且开放的问题。

Method: 引入了切锥上的最小单位球方向导数φ(x)与全局斜率建立精确联系；利用方向导数与切锥建立全局和局部误差界的充分必要条件；通过摄动稳定性、子水平集的Hausdorff稳定性及逆子水平集估计建立等价刻画；在有限维空间中推导尖锐的局部结果和最优常数1/φ(x̄)。

Result: 建立了非负下半连续函数全局误差界的若干等价条件；证明了Lipschitz函数中sup_{x∉S0}φ(x)<0是误差界的充分条件，凸函数中也是必要条件；在有限维中，若φ(x̄)>0则局部误差界成立且最优常数为1/φ(x̄)，若φ(x̄)=0且在解集切锥外存在合适方向则局部误差界失败。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般非凸非光滑函数的误差界理论提供了统一且精确的刻画框架，特别是方向导数与切锥相结合的几何视角，揭示了误差界成立与否的本质条件。

Abstract: We develop new characterizations of both global and local error bounds for general functions, using directional derivatives and tangent cones without imposing convexity or linear structure. We first establish several equivalent conditions for the global error bound of a nonnegative lower semicontinuous function. These equivalences hold for general, possibly nonconvex and nonsmooth functions. We further link the error bound with perturbation stability, Hausdorff stability of sublevel sets, and an inverse-sublevel-set estimate. Turning to directional derivatives, we introduce the minimal unit-sphere directional derivative \(\varphi(x)\) on the tangent cone and clarify its exact relation with the global slope. For Lipschitz continuous functions we prove that \(\sup_{x\notin S_0} \varphi(x) < 0\) is sufficient for an error bound, and for convex functions on convex sets this condition is also necessary, In finite dimensions we obtain sharp local results: if \(\varphi(\bar{x}) > 0\) at a solution \(\bar{x}\), then a local error bound holds and the optimal constant is exactly \(1/\varphi(\bar{x})\); if \(\varphi(\bar{x}) = 0\) and a suitable direction exists outside the tangent cone of the solution set, the local error bound fails. A general estimate relating the directional derivative to the distance from the tangent cone of the solution set is also derived.

</details>


### [128] [A Policy Iteration Scheme for Semilinear Stochastic Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman Equations with Exponential Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29024)
*Hasib Uddin Molla,Jinniao Qiu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题，提出了一种基于逐次线性化的策略迭代算法，将非线性随机Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman（SHJB）方程转化为一列线性方程，并证明了近似序列以指数速率在均方意义下单调收敛到值函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题中，值函数是由SHJB方程刻画的随机场。当随机积分系数不受控制时，SHJB方程呈半线性形式，由于存在可测随机性，其数值求解相比马尔可夫情形更具计算挑战性。

Method: 提出一种基于逐次线性化的策略迭代算法，将非线性SHJB方程转化为一序列线性SHJB方程进行求解。

Result: 证明了策略迭代产生的近似序列在均方意义下以指数速率单调收敛到值函数。

Conclusion: 该策略迭代算法为半线性SHJB方程提供了一种收敛速度快、具有理论保证的数值求解途径，有效应对了非马尔可夫情形下由于可测随机性带来的计算困难。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the non-Markovian stochastic optimal control problems in which the value function is a random field characterized by a stochastic Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (SHJB) equation. When the stochastic integration coefficients are not controlled, the SHJB equation takes a semilinear form, which is subject to computational challenges compared to the Markovian case due to the measurable randomness. We introduce a policy-iteration algorithm based on successive linearization that reduces the nonlinear SHJB equation to a sequence of linear ones. Furthermore, we prove that the resulting approximation sequence converges monotonically to the value function in the mean-square sense with an exponential rate.

</details>


### [129] [Baseball, An Extensive-Form Game-Theoretic Duel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29041)
*Sebastian E. Ferrando,Eli Kohn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将棒球打席建模为有限二人零和、含机会节点与不完全信息的扩展式博弈，采用序列式（sequence-form）表示与线性规划求解最小最大均衡，从MLB Statcast数据估计自然转移概率，并证明了序列式对偶变量的动态规划解释及在状态马尔可夫假设下可用十二个非终结球数的反向归纳精确计算均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棒球打席涉及投手与打者之间的策略对抗，传统统计或简化模型难以刻画逐球博弈中的不完全信息与策略互动。论文希望通过博弈论框架为打席策略提供精确的均衡解，并克服正规式（normal form）博弈树规模爆炸的实际计算瓶颈。

Method: 将每个打席建模为带机会节点与不完全信息的有限二人零和扩展式博弈；采用序列式表示法使线性规划规模随博弈树线性增长，从而支持远超出正规式可行范围的大型博弈树精确最小最大均衡求解；利用MLB Statcast数据估计自然（机会）转移概率；进一步在一般完美回忆有限零和博弈中证明序列式最佳响应程序的对偶变量可分解为到达权重与条件延续值，并在状态马尔可夫假设下证明可对十二个非终结计数进行反向归纳求解完整均衡。

Result: 作者导出了两种互补的动态规划解释，建立了序列式对偶变量与到达权重、条件延续值的对应关系，并证明了在棒球模型的状态马尔可夫假设下，完整最小最大均衡可通过十二个非终结球数上的反向归纳高效计算。计算输出涵盖均衡策略、延续值，以及非最优行动在条件与到达加权两种口径下的策略成本度量。

Conclusion: 该工作为棒球打席提供了可扩展的精确博弈论求解框架，兼具理论深度（序列式形式与动态规划的联系）与应用价值（基于真实Statcast数据的均衡策略与战略成本量化），为后续投打博弈分析与策略优化奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: We formulate a baseball plate appearance as a finite two-person, zero-sum, extensive-form game with chance moves and imperfect information. Successive pitch cycles are connected through the evolving count, while the Batter chooses an action without observing the Pitcher's current pitch selection. Terminal outcomes are valued through a run-expectancy utility that incorporates both runs scored during the plate appearance and the continuation value of the half-inning. We implement the sequence-form representation of the game, whose linear-programming formulation grows linearly with the game tree and permits exact minimax equilibrium computations for trees far beyond the practical range of the normal form. Nature's transition probabilities are estimated from MLB Statcast data. We also establish two complementary dynamic-programming interpretations. For general finite two-person zero-sum games with perfect recall, we show that the dual variables of the sequence-form best-response programs decompose into reach weights and conditional continuation values. Under the additional state-Markov assumptions of the baseball model, we prove that the full minimax equilibrium can be computed by backward induction over the twelve non-terminal counts. The resulting computations produce equilibrium strategies, continuation values, and conditional and reach-weighted measures of the strategic cost of non-optimal actions.

</details>


### [130] [Newton Method for Fixed-Support Doubly Entropic Wasserstein Barycenter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29109)
*Jianting Pan,Sirong Dai,Lei Yang,Yaomin Wang,Ji'an Li,Ming Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究固定支撑的双正则化Wasserstein重心问题，通过熵最优传输的半对偶形式将其转化为光滑无约束凸优化问题，推导了梯度和Hessian的显式表达式，提出了精确Newton方法和稀疏Newton变体以加速高精度重心计算，并在理论上给出了Hessian逼近界和收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein重心在诸多领域（如图像处理、形状分析、统计推断）中具有重要应用，但高精度计算面临计算代价高昂的挑战。已有方法在精度和可扩展性之间难以兼顾，需要开发兼具高精度与高可扩展性的求解算法。

Method: 利用熵最优传输的半对偶公式，将双正则化Wasserstein重心问题转化为关于对偶变量的光滑无约束凸优化问题；推导梯度与Hessian的显式闭式表达式；提出精确Newton方法以实现高精度求解；进一步提出稀疏Newton变体，通过对传输概率矩阵进行稀疏化（sparsification）来降低Hessian-向量乘积的计算成本。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，稀疏Newton方法相比现有方法收敛更快；理论上建立了Hessian近似误差界以及所提出方法的收敛性保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的Newton类方法能够在保持高精度的同时有效提升Wasserstein重心计算的规模可扩展性，为大规模高精度重心计算提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: We study the fixed-support doubly regularized Wasserstein barycenter problem. Using the semi-dual formulation of entropic optimal transport, we reformulate the problem as a smooth, unconstrained, convex optimization problem in the dual variables. We then derive explicit expressions for the gradient and Hessian and develop an exact Newton method for high-accuracy barycenter computation. To improve scalability, we propose a sparse Newton variant that sparsifies the transport probability matrices, thereby reducing the cost of Hessian-vector products. We establish theoretical results for the proposed methods, including Hessian approximation bounds and convergence results. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that the sparse Newton method converges faster than

</details>


### [131] [Anisotropic Higher-Order Semiregularity of Degenerate Generalized Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29114)
*Tomáš Roubal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自包含的各向异性高阶覆盖与逆估计的三角化框架，适用于光滑映射和广义方程，通过三角因子条件与p-核条件建立分块覆盖与逆估计，并利用闭凸过程内逼近处理集值项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的高阶覆盖与逆估计方法在处理各向异性（即不同方向/分块具有不同阶数）问题时缺乏统一的、自包含的表述。本文旨在填补这一空白，为光滑映射和广义方程提供统一的三角化分析框架。

Method: (1) 对C^p映射固定指标化的目标分解，施加三角因子条件并选取满足三角p-核条件的方向；(2) 通过三角p-因子算子的满射性获得固定尺度的包含关系，每个非零块Y_i以t^i阶被覆盖，从而得到该块上指数为1/i的逆估计；(3) 对广义方程，先证明一个辅助的精确模型定理以隔离值域传递机制（带无环修正纤维），再使用闭凸过程内逼近与光滑三角因子算子的和满足满射性作为主要充分判据。

Result: 建立了块状固定尺度包含关系，每块Y_i以t^i阶覆盖且具有指数1/i的逆估计；零块被允许且p为最高活跃等级。光滑结果为导数级的分级表述，可直接从C^p导数验证模型。广义方程情形通过精确模型定理和内逼近判据，使集值项补充缺失方向。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一个统一的、自包含的各向异性高阶三角化框架，同时处理光滑映射和广义方程，为不同分块提供不同阶数的覆盖与逆估计，并允许零块的存在，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We give a self-contained triangular formulation of anisotropic higher-order
  covering and inverse estimates for smooth mappings and generalized equations.
  For a $C^p$ mapping into a finite-dimensional target, an indexed target
  decomposition is fixed, the corresponding triangular factor condition is
  imposed, and a direction satisfying the associated triangular $p$-kernel
  condition is chosen. Surjectivity of the resulting triangular $p$-factor
  operator yields a fixed-scale inclusion in which each nonzero block $Y_i$ is
  covered at order $t^i$, and hence an inverse estimate with exponent $1/i$ on
  that block. Zero blocks are allowed, and $p$ denotes the highest active grade.
  The smooth result is a derivative-level graded formulation: it verifies the
  model directly from the $C^p$ derivatives and records the blockwise
  fixed-scale inclusion needed for the set-valued analysis.
  For generalized equations, an auxiliary exact-model theorem isolates the
  range-transfer mechanism with acyclic correction fibres. The main sufficient
  criterion uses a closed convex-process inner approximation whose sum with
  the smooth triangular factor operator is surjective, allowing the set-valued
  term to supply missing directions.

</details>


### [132] [Robust Airfoil Design Optimization via a Bilevel Model-Based Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29161)
*Alpaslan Kurt,Dilara Buk,Yusuf Yazicioglu,Figen Oztoprak,Onur Son,Gorkem Demir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GLORO的双层求解方法，用于处理涉及昂贵函数评估的鲁棒优化问题。该方法结合了参数空间中的高斯过程代理模型（通过贝叶斯优化进行下层优化）和变量空间中的局部模型（用于上层优化），并应用于RAE2822翼型在不确定马赫数和攻角下的鲁棒升阻比优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒优化问题通常涉及昂贵的函数评估，传统方法计算开销巨大。为了在保证优化效果的同时提升计算效率，需要一种能够将昂贵评估引导到优化关键区域的智能方法。

Method: GLORO采用双层（bilevel）求解架构：下层在参数空间中利用高斯过程代理模型结合贝叶斯优化来近似计算鲁棒优化问题的目标函数和约束；上层在变量空间中利用这些近似评估构建局部模型进行优化。两层设计均旨在将昂贵的函数评估集中引导到优化过程真正感兴趣的区域，从而减少不必要的计算开销。

Result: 该方法在鲁棒翼型设计优化问题中进行了测试，具体针对RAE2822翼型，在来流马赫数和攻角存在运行不确定性的条件下优化翼型形状以获得鲁棒的升阻比。

Conclusion: GLORO通过参数空间的贝叶斯优化与变量空间的局部模型相结合的双层策略，为昂贵函数评估的鲁棒优化问题提供了一种计算上高效且优化的求解方案，并在实际翼型设计中得到验证。

Abstract: We propose GLORO, a bilevel solution methodology for robust optimization involving expensive function evaluations. The methodology is carefully designed to achieve satisfactory optimization results in a computationally efficient manner. It is based on Gaussian Process surrogates in the parameter space for approximate computations of the objective / constraints of the robust optimization problem via Bayesian optimization (lower level), and local models constructed in the variable space using these approximate evaluations (upper level). Both the use of Bayesian optimization (in parameter space) and the use of local models (in variable space) are motivated by the idea of guiding the expensive function evaluations to the regions of interest for the optimization process. The methodological work is motivated by and tested on a robust airfoil design optimization problem. This application focuses on the RAE2822 airfoil, optimizing its shape to ensure a robust lift-to-drag ratio under operational uncertainties in Mach number and angle of attack.

</details>


### [133] [Volume of quasi-homogeneous sublevel sets: Two linear algebra deterministic algorithms with convergence rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29269)
*Didier Henrion,Jean B Lasserre*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出两种确定性算法来计算正拟齐次多项式单位亚水平集的Lebesgue体积。通过将维度压缩到单变量矩问题，算法分别基于Chebyshev多项式逼近（余弦变换，多项式收敛）和广义特征值问题（指数收敛），两种算法均绕过半定优化，仅依赖数值线性代数，并可为多元moment-SOS体积层级提供可行解，改善其已知收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算多项式亚水平集体积是多项式优化和几何中的核心问题，传统方法受限于环境维度，且基于半定规划的松弛在大规模问题中计算代价高昂。作者希望消除环境维度对优化的影响，并设计不依赖半定优化、具有明确收敛保证的确定性算法。

Method: 首先将沿边界框的Lebesgue测度通过正拟齐次多项式前推，将高维体积约化为单变量矩问题。维度仅出现在预处理阶段（计算多项式在框上的矩，对稀疏或可分离多项式而言计算量为关于维度的多项式）。随后提出两种单变量松弛的确定性算法：第一种用Chebyshev多项式逼近分段常数函数，每次松弛化为快速余弦变换；第二种从仅含矩矩阵和局部化矩阵的广义特征值问题中提取体积界，其矩阵规模随松弛阶线性增长，收敛速率为指数级。

Result: 两种算法均返回体积的认证上界和下界。Chebyshev逼近法以多项式速率收敛，GEVP法以指数速率收敛，其中指数速率由多项式在边界框上的先验上界决定。两种算法产生的单变量多项式对多元moment-SOS体积层级是可行的，因此代数与几何收敛速率可传递给整个层级，优于其已知的最佳收敛速率。

Conclusion: 通过将高维体积问题压缩到单变量域，并利用Chebyshev逼近和广义特征值分解两种仅依赖数值线性代数的技术，本文提供了兼具计算效率、收敛保证和层级兼容性的体积计算方法，显著推进了moment-SOS层级在体积问题上的理论基础。

Abstract: We consider the problem of computing the Lebesgue volume of the unit sublevel set of a positive quasi-homogeneous polynomial. Pushing the Lebesgue measure of an ambient bounding box forward through the polynomial reduces this high-dimensional volume to a one-dimensional moment problem. This removes the ambient dimension from the optimization and confines the dimension to a single preprocessing stage, computing the moments of the polynomial over the box, which is polynomial in the ambient dimension for sparse or separable polynomials. We propose two deterministic algorithms for the resulting univariate relaxations, each returning certified upper and lower bounds on the volume. Both bypass semidefinite optimization entirely and rely only on standard numerical linear algebra. The first approximates a piecewise-constant function by a Chebyshev polynomial, so that each relaxation reduces to a fast cosine transform, and converges at a polynomial rate in the relaxation order. The second extracts the volume bounds from a single generalized eigenvalue problem involving moment and localizing matrices whose size grows linearly with the relaxation order, and converges at an exponential rate; the ratio governing this rate is determined by an a priori upper bound on the polynomial over the bounding box. Finally, the univariate polynomials produced by either algorithm are feasible for the multivariate moment-SOS volume hierarchy. The algebraic and geometric rates therefore transfer to the hierarchy itself, improving on its best known convergence rates.

</details>


### [134] [Rational Jacobi Rotations and the Complexity of Approximating Mixed Integer Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29386)
*Alberto Del Pia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在图灵机上以多项式时间求解混合整数二次规划（MIQP）ε-近似解的算法，前提是整数变量个数和Hessian负特征值个数固定。除非P=NP，这两个限制都是必要的，从而完整刻画了MIQP近似的复杂度。核心工具是一个多项式时间的同步对角化算法，利用有理Jacobi旋转将二次型化为例外量任意小的可分形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数二次规划（MIQP）的近似求解复杂度此前尚未被完整刻画。已有方法要么需要无理坐标变换（无法在图灵机上高效执行），要么对问题限制过于严格。作者希望填补这一空白，特别关注连续情形下也尚未解决的近似问题。

Method: 提出一个多项式时间的同步对角化算法：计算有理基变换，将给定（以因子化形式呈现的）椭球精确映射为球，同时使目标函数在任意小扰动范围内变为可分的，并保持Hessian的惯性。与传统构造（需要无理变换使目标函数精确可分）不同，该方法基于有理Jacobi旋转。在此基础上，结合固定维度的整数变量枚举或分支策略，实现在图灵机上多项式时间内得到ε-近似解。

Result: （1）在整数变量个数和Hessian负特征值个数均为常数时，存在多项式时间（在实例规模和1/ε意义上）的MIQP ε-近似算法；（2）除非P=NP，这两项限制均不可放宽，因此完整刻画了MIQP近似的复杂度；（3）该结果即使在纯连续情形下也是全新的；（4）所提出的有理Jacobi旋转方法本身具有独立的理论价值。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于有理Jacobi旋转的同步对角化技术，本文完整刻画了MIQP近似求解的复杂度边界：固定整数变量数和Hessian负惯性即可实现多项式时间的ε-近似，且这个条件是紧的（除非P=NP）。该结果同时推广了连续情形下的已知结果，并为后续基于有理正交变换的优化算法提供了新工具。

Abstract: We present an algorithm that finds an epsilon-approximate solution to a mixed integer quadratic programming (MIQP) problem, and that runs on a Turing machine in time polynomial in the size of the instance and in 1/epsilon, provided that the number of integer variables and the number of negative eigenvalues of the Hessian of the objective function are fixed. Unless P=NP, both restrictions are necessary, so this completes the characterization of the complexity of approximating MIQP in terms of the number of integer variables and the inertia of the Hessian; the result is new already in the purely continuous case. The main ingredient is a polynomial-time simultaneous diagonalization algorithm: it computes a rational change of basis that maps a given ellipsoid, presented in factored form, exactly to a ball, while making the objective function separable up to an arbitrarily small perturbation and preserving the inertia of its Hessian.
